passIndex.value))\n\nconst groupDoneCount = computed(() => (showGroupIntro.value ? 0 : runner.inGroupDone.value))\nconst groupTotalCount = computed(() => {\n  if (showGroupIntro.value && computed(() => `${groupDoneCount.value} / ${groupTotalCount.value || '—'}`)\nconst inGroupPct = computed(() => {\n  const t = groupTotalCount.value || 0\n  if (!t) return 0\n  return Math.min(100, Math.round((100 * groupDoneCount.value) / t))\n})\n\nconst comparisonsDone = computed(() => {\n  return Number(progress.value.participant_comparisons_done || 0) + (showGroupIntro.value ? 0 : runner.inGroupDone.value)\n})\nconst minTarget = computed(() => Number(progress.value.estimated_comparisons_at_min || 0))\nconst pastMinTarget = computed(() => {\n  const minPasses = Number(progress.value.min_expected_passes || 0)\n  const completed = Number(progress.value.passes_completed || 0)\n  if (minPasses > 0 && completed >= minPasses) return true\n  return minTarget.value > 0 && comparisonsDone.value >= minTarget.value\n})\nconst overallPct = computed(() => {\n  if (pastMinTarget.value) return 100\n  const total = minTarget.value\n  if (!total) return 0\n  return || {}\n  const maxP = Number(p.max_recommended_passes || projectSettings.value.max_recommended_passes || 0)\n  const completed = Number(p.passes_completed || 0)\n  if (!maxP || completed < maxP) return false\n  const g = res.next_group\n  if (!g) return true\n  return Number(g.pass_index || 0) > maxP\n}\n\nfunction beginGroup(g: SortGroupPayload) {\n  heldNextGroup.value = null\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  lastFlushedCount.value = 0\n  if (multiFactorMode.value) {\n    pendingGroup.value = g\n    showGroupIntro.value = true\n    runner.stop()\n  } else {\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    runner.startGroup(g)\n  }\n}\n\nfunction beginPendingGroup() {\n  const g = pendingGroup.value\n  if (!g) return\n  showGroupIntro.value = false\n  pendingGroup.value = null\n  runner.startGroup(g)\n}\n\nfunction applyPayload(res: any) {\n  if (res.failure_reason) {\n    error.value = String(res.failure_reason)\n    return\n  }\n  error.value = ''\n  if (res.project) {\n    projectTitle.value = res.project.project_title || res.project.project_tag || 'Project'\n    if (res.project.factor_count != null) ranking.setCatalog(\n    (res.alternatives || []).map((a: any) => Number(a.id)).filter(Boolean),\n    (res.factors || []).map((f: any) => Number(f.id)).filter(Boolean),\n  )\n  projectSettings.value = applyPayload(res)\n    if (res.personal_vote?.is_complete && shareKind.value === 'vote_view') {\n      completeView.value = true\n      votesCaptured.value = Number(res.votes_captured || = null\n      maxCompleteView.value = false\n      runner.stop()\n    }\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    gateMessage.value = e?.message || 'Access failed'\n    gateMessageType.value = 'error'\n  } finally {\n    gateLoading.value = false\n    loading.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function submitCompletedGroup() {\n  const pkg = runner.buildPackage()\n  if (!pkg) return\n  fetching.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await shares.extCompleteGroup(token.value, {\n      ...gateCreds.value,\n      share_kind: shareKind.value,\n      prior_group: pkg,\n    }) as any\n    applyPayload(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to save group'\n  } finally {\n    fetching.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nwatch(\n  () => runner.complete.value,\n  async (done) => {\n    if (done && runner.group.value) await submitCompletedGroup()\n  },\n)\n\nconst lastFlushedCount = ref(0)\nlet idleTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null\n\nfunction flushThresholds() {\n  return {\n    count: Math.max(1, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_flush_count || 5)),\n    idleMs: Math.max(5, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_idle_seconds || 90)) * 1000,\n  }\n}\n\nasync function flushPartial() {\n  if (runner.complete.value || submitting.value) return\n  const pkg = runner.buildPartialPackage()\n  if (!pkg || !pkg.pairings.length) return\n  if (pkg.pairings.length === lastFlushedCount.value) return\n  try {\n    await shares.extSaveGroup(token.value, {\n      ...gateCreds.value,\n      share_kind: shareKind.value,\n      prior_group: pkg,\n    })\n    lastFlushedCount.value = pkg.pairings.length\n  } catch { /* best-effort */ }\n}\n\nwatch(\n  () => [runner.group.value, runner.pairings.value, runner.inGroupDone.value] as const,\n  () => ranking.setLive(runner.group.value, runner.pairings.value),\n)\n\nwatch(\n  () => runner.inGroupDone.value,\n  (n) => {\n    if (idleTimer) clearTimeout(idleTimer)\n    if (!n || }, idleMs)\n  },\n)\n\nonBeforeUnmount(() => {\n  if (idleTimer) clearTimeout(idleTimer)\n})\n\nfunction onPause() {\n  paused.value = true\n  runner.setPaused(true)\n}\nfunction onResume() {\n  paused.value = false\n  runner.setPaused(false)\n}\n\nasync function continuePastMax() {\n  maxCompleteAcknowledged.value = true\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  const held = heldNextGroup.value\n  fetching.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function markComplete() {\n  submitting.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await shares.extComplete(token.value, {\n      ...gateCreds.value,\n </template>\n585:   </div>\n586: </template>\n587: \n588: <script setup lang=\"ts\">\n589: import type { VoteParticipant } from '~/types/api'\n590: import {\n591:   formatPercent,\n592:   formatNullablePercent,\n593:   formatRankCi,\n594:   formatWeightCi,\n595:   formatExpectedRank,\n596:   scoreBarPercent,\n597:   scoreLeadPoints,\n598:   asMetricPercent,\n599: polarizingCaption,\n606:   insightMetricChips,\n607:   lookupRankMap,\n608:   rankingFromMeanRankMap,\n609:   participantLeaderUnderFactor,\n610:   participantLeadersUnderFactor,\n611:   tiedLeadersFromRanking,\n612: } from '~/utils/ranking'\n613: \n614: const route = useRoute()\n615: const snackbar = useSnackbar()\n616: const votes = useVotesApi()\n617: const { isAdmin, aiFeaturesEnabled, requireAdmin } = useAuth()\n618: const runningAi = ref(false)\n619: const ws = useProjectWorkspace()\n620: \n621: async function runAiBaseline() {\n622:   if (!requireAdmin() || !project.value) return\n623:   runningAi.value = true\n624:   try {\n625:     const res = await votes.runAiBaseline(project.value.id)\n626:     if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n627:     if (res.already_complete) snackbar.success('AI agents have already finished')\n628:     else snackbar.success('AI agents started. Refresh results when they finish.')\n629:   }\n630:   catch (e: unknown) {\n631:     snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not start AI baseline')\n632:   }\n633:   finally {\n634:     runningAi.value = false\n635:   }\n636: }\n637: const {\n638:   project, altItems, factorItems, participants, report,\n639:   loading, settings, loadProject,\n640: } = ws\n641: \n642: const analysisMode = ref<'human' | 'agents' | 'combined'>('human')\n643: const canRunAiAgents = computed(() =>\n644:   !!aiFeaturesEnabled.value\n645:   && isAdmin.value\n646:   && !!project.value?.include_ai_agents\n647:   && (project.value?.ai_voter_models || []).length > 0,\n648: )\n649: const hasAgentAnalysis = computed(() => {\n650:   const analysis = report.value?.analysis as Record<string, any> | undefined\n651:   return !!(analysis?.agents || report.value?.ai_baseline?.available)\n652: })\n653: const viewReport = computed(() => {\n654:   const base = report.value || {}\n655:   const analysis = (base as any).analysis as Record<string, any> | undefined\n656:   if (!analysis) return base\n657:   if (analysisMode.value === 'agents' && analysis.agents) return { ...base, ...analysis.agents }\n658:   if (analysisMode.value === 'combined' && analysis.combined) return { ...base, ...analysis.combined }\n659:   if (analysis.human) return { ...base, ...analysis.human }\n660:   return base\n661: })\n662: const optionAgentComparison = computed(() => {\n663:   const analysis = report.value?.analysis as Record<string, any> | undefined\n664:   return (analysis?.comparison?.option_ranking as any[]) || []\n665: })\n666: const factorAgentComparison = computed(() => {\n667:   const analysis = report.value?.analysis as Record<string, any> | undefined\n668:   return (analysis?.comparison?.factor_ranking as any[]) || []\n669: })\n670: const resultWeighting = ref('adjusted')\n671: const leaderWeighting = ref('adjusted')\n672: const selectedParticipant = ref<VoteParticipant | null>(null)\n673: const factorSortKey = ref<'rank' | 'importance' | 'discrimination' | 'leverage'>('rank')\n674: \n675: const pivotAxis = ref<'alternatives' | 'factors' | 'participants'>('participants')\n676: const pivotSortKey = ref('rank')\n677: const filterAlternativeId = ref<number | null>(null)\n678: const filterFactorId = ref<number | null>(null)\n679: const filterParticipantId = ref<number | null>(null)\n680: const selectedPivotRowId = ref<number | null>(null)\n681: const pivotDetail = ref<Record<string, any> | null>(null)\n682: const pivotDetailLoading = ref(false)\n683: \n684: const influenceModeLabel = computed(() => {\n685:   const mode = normalizeParticipantInfluenceMode(project.value?.participant_influence_mode)\n686:   if (mode === 'balanced') return 'Balanced influence'\n687:   if (mode === 'participants_normalized') return 'Participants normalized'\n688:   return 'Comparisons equal'\n689: })\n690: \n691: const metrics = computed(() => {\n692:   const src = viewReport.value as Record<string, any>\n693:   const m = (src?.metrics as Record<string, any>) || src || {}\n694:   return {\n695:     stability: asMetricPercent(m.stability ?? src?.stability),\n696:     confidence: asMetricPercent(m.confidence ?? src?.confidence),\n697:     completion: asMetricPercent(m.completion ?? src?.completion),\n698:     coverage: asMetricPercent(m.coverage ?? 0),\n699:     agreement: asMetricPercent(m.agreement ?? src?.agreement),\n700:     multi_pass: asMetricPercent(m.multi_pass ?? m.inter_group ?? src?.multi_pass ?? 0),\n701:     factor_divergence: asMetricPercent(m.factor_divergence ?? src?.factor_divergence ?? 0),\n702:     inter_group: asMetricPercent(m.inter_group ?? m.multi_pass ?? 0),\n703:     depth: asMetricPercent(m.depth ?? 0),\n704:     stability_basis: String(m.stability_basis ?? src?.stability_basis ?? ''),\n705:   }\n706: })\n707: \n708: const comparisonCount = computed(() =>\n709:   Number(viewReport.value?.comparison_count ?? viewReport.value?.total_observations ?? viewReport.value?.observation_count ?? 0),\n710: )\n711: \n712: const rankingMode = computed(() =>\n713:   normalizeRankingMode(report.value?.ranking_mode, project.value?.project_exclusive_mode),\n714: )\n715: \n716: const reportTopN = computed(() => Number(report.value?.top_n) || 1)\n717: \n718: const heroHeadline = computed(() => influenceModeLabel }}</span>\n                <span class=\"factor-tag\">Score {{ formatPercent(displayRankings[0].score) }}</span>\n                <span v-if=\"displayRankings.length > 1\" class=\"factor-tag\">Lead {{ leaderGap }} pts</span>\n                <span class=\"factor-tag\">{{ uniqueParticipants }} participants</span>\n                <span class=\"factor-tag\">{{ comparisonCount }} comparisons</span>\n              </div>\n            </v-col>\n          </v-row>\n        </v-card-text>\n      </v-card>\n\n      <v-row dense class=\"mb-5\">\n        <v-col v-for=\"chip in insightChips\" :key=\"`card-${chip.key}`\" cols=\"12\" sm=\"4\">\n          <v-card class=\"glass-card h-100\"><v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n            <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">{{ chip.label }}</div>\n            <div class=\"text-h3 font-weight-bold\">{{ chip.display }}</div>\n            <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-1\">{{ chip.caption }}</div>\n          </v-card-text></v-card>\n        </v-col>\n      </v-row>\n\n      <v-row>\n        <v-col cols=\"12\" lg=\"7\">\n          <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5 pa-md-6\">\n              <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-3 align-center mb-5\">\n                <div>\n                  <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold\" style=\"font-family:Manrope,Inter,sans-serif\">Overall ranking</div>\n                  <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">{{ resultViewExplanation }}</div>\n                </div>\n                <v-select\n                  v-model=\"resultWeighting\"\n                  :items=\"resultViewOptions\"\n                  item-title=\"title\"\n                  item-value=\"value\"\n                  label=\"View results by\"\n                  density=\"compact\"\n                  hide-details\n                  style=\"max-width:280px\"\n                />\n              </div>\n              <div v-if=\"displayRankings.length\" class=\"d-flex flex-column ga-3\">\n                <div v-for=\"(item, index) in displayRankings\" :key=\"item.id\" class=\"rank-row\">\n                  <div class=\"d-flex align-center ga-3\">\n                    <div class=\"rank-number\">{{ index + 1 }}</div>\n                    <div class=\"flex-grow-1\">\n                      <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between ga-3\">\n                        <strong>{{ item.title }}</strong>\n                        <span class=\"font-weight-bold\">{{ formatPercent(item.score) }}</span>\n                      </div>\n                      <v-progress-linear :model-value=\"scoreBarPercent(item.score)\" height=\"8\" rounded color=\"primary\" class=\"mt-2\" />\n                      <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-2 personal-rank-caption\">\n                        <RankRangeBar\n                          :expected-rank=\"item.expected_rank\"\n                          :rank-sd=\"item.rank_sd\"\n                          :rank-ci95=\"item.rank_ci95\"\n                          :item-count=\"displayRankings.length\"\n                          density=\"comfortable\"\n                        >\n                          <template #fallback>\n                            <template v-if=\"formatRankCi(item.rank_ci95)\">{{ formatRankCi(item.rank_ci95) }}</template>\n                            <template v-else-if=\"formatExpectedRank(item.expected_rank)\">\n                              expected rank {{ formatExpectedRank(item.expected_rank) }}\n                            </template>\n                            <template v-else>\n                              Mean rank {{ item.mean_rank != null ? Number(item.mean_rank).toFixed(2) : '—' }}\n                            </template>\n                            <template v-if=\"rankingMode === 'rank_all' && formatExpectedRank(item.expected_rank) && formatRankCi(item.rank_ci95)\">\n                              · expected {{ formatExpectedRank(item.expected_rank) }}\n                            </template>\n                          </template>\n                        </RankRangeBar>\n                        <template v-if=\"optionChanceCaption(item, rankingMode, reportTopN)\">\n                          {{ optionChanceCaption(item, rankingMode, reportTopN) }}\n                        </template>\n                        <span>· {{ item.data_points ?? item.evidence ?? 0 }} groups</span>\n                      </div>\n                      <div\n                        v-if=\"polarizingCaption(item)\"\n                        class=\"text-caption mt-1\"\n                      >\n                        {{ polarizingCaption(item) }}\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <div v-else class=\"empty-state py-10\">\n                <div class=\"font-weight-bold\">Start comparisons to establish a ranking</div>\n              </div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n\n          <v-card v-if=\"factorRankings.length > 1\" class=\"glass-card mt-5\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5 pa-md-6\">\n              <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-3 align-center mb-3\">\n                <div>\n                  <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold\">What matters most</div>\n                  <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">\n                    Importance is how much the factor matters. Differentiation is how much it separates options. Decision leverage is which factors actually move the outcome.\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n                <v-btn-toggle v-model=\"factorSortKey\" mandatory density=\"compact\" color=\"primary\" variant=\"outlined\" divided>\n                  <v-btn value=\"rank\" size=\"small\">Rank</v-btn>\n                  <v-btn value=\"importance\" size=\"small\">Importance</v-btn>\n                  <v-btn value=\"discrimination\" size=\"small\">Differentiation</v-btn>\n                  <v-btn value=\"leverage\" size=\"small\">Leverage</v-btn>\n                </v-btn-toggle>\n              </div>\n              <div v-for=\"(criterion, index) in sortedFactorRankings\" :key=\"criterion.id\" class=\"rank-row mb-3\">\n                <div class=\"d-flex align-center ga-3\">\n                  <div class=\"rank-number\">{{ index + 1 }}</div>\n                  <div class=\"flex-grow-1\">\n                    <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between ga-3\">\n                      <strong>{{ criterion.title }}</strong>\n                      <span class=\"font-weight-bold\">{{ formatNullablePercent(criterion.normalizedWeight ?? criterion.weight ?? criterion.score) }}</span>\n                    </div>\n                    <v-progress-linear\n                      :model-value=\"scoreBarPercent(criterion.normalizedWeight ?? criterion.weight ?? criterion.score ?? 0)\"\n                      height=\"8\"\n                      rounded\n                      color=\"deep-purple\"\n                      class=\"mt-2\"\n                    />\n                    <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-2 factor-violin-row\">\n                      <span class=\"violin-label\">Importance</span>\n                      <WeightRangeBar\n                        :weight=\"criterion.normalizedWeight ?? criterion.weight ?? criterion.score\"\n                        :weight-ci95=\"criterion.weight_ci95\"\n                        density=\"comfortable\"\n                      >\n                        <template #fallback>\n                          <span>\n                            Importance {{ formatNullablePercent(criterion.normalizedWeight ?? criterion.weight ?? criterion.score) }}\n                            <template v-if=\"formatWeightCi(criterion.weight_ci95)\">\n                              ({{ formatWeightCi(criterion.weight_ci95) }})\n                            </template>\n                          </span>\n                        </template>\n                      </WeightRangeBar>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-1 factor-violin-row\">\n                      <span class=\"violin-label\">Rank</span>\n                      <RankRangeBar\n                        :expected-rank=\"criterion.expected_rank\"\n                        :rank-sd=\"criterion.rank_sd\"\n                        :rank-ci95=\"criterion.rank_ci95\"\n                        :item-count=\"sortedFactorRankings.length\"\n                        density=\"comfortable\"\n                      >\n                        <template #fallback>\n                          <span v-if=\"formatRankCi(criterion.rank_ci95)\">{{ formatRankCi(criterion.rank_ci95) }}</span>\n                          <span v-else-if=\"formatExpectedRank(criterion.expected_rank)\">expected rank {{ formatExpectedRank(criterion.expected_rank) }}</span>\n                          <span v-else>Rank {{ criterion.rank ?? index + 1 }}</span>\n                        </template>\n                      </RankRangeBar>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-4 text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-2\">\n                      <span>Differentiation {{ formatNullablePercent(criterion.discrimination) }}</span>\n                      <span>Decision leverage {{ formatNullablePercent(criterion.leverage) }}</span>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n\n          <v-card v-if=\"analysisMode === 'combined' && optionAgentComparison.length\" class=\"glass-card mt-5\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5 pa-md-6\">\n              <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold mb-1\">Agents vs humans — option ranking</div>\n              <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-4\">\n                Rank delta is human rank minus agent rank. Positive means agents ranked the option higher.\n              </div>\n              <div v-for=\"row in optionAgentComparison\" :key=\"`cmp-opt-${row.id}`\" class=\"rank-row mb-2\">\n                <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between ga-3\">\n                  <strong>{{ row.title }}</strong>\n                  <span class=\"text-caption\">\n                    Human {{ row.human_rank ?? '—' }} · Agent {{ row.agent_rank ?? '—' }}\n                    <template v-if=\"row.rank_delta != null\"> · Δ {{ row.rank_delta > 0 ? '+' : '' }}{{ row.rank_delta }}</template>\n                  </span>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n\n          <v-card v-if=\"analysisMode === 'combined' && factorAgentComparison.length\" class=\"glass-card mt-5\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5 pa-md-6\">\n              <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold mb-1\">Agents vs humans — factor ranking</div>\n              <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-4\">\n                How AI agents ordered factors compared with human participants.\n              </div>\n              <div v-for=\"row in factorAgentComparison\" :key=\"`cmp-fac-${row.id}`\" class=\"rank-row mb-2\">\n                <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between ga-3\">\n                  <strong>{{ row.title }}</strong>\n                  <span class=\"text-caption\">\n                    Human {{ row.human_rank ?? '—' }} · Agent {{ row.agent_rank ?? '—' }}\n                    <template v-if=\"row.rank_delta != null\"> · Δ {{ row.rank_delta > 0 ? '+' : '' }}{{ row.rank_delta }}</template>\n                  </span>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n\n          <v-card v-if=\"perCriterionLeaders.length\" class=\"glass-card mt-5\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n              <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold mb-1\">Best option by factor</div>\n              <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-4\">\n                Mean rank across passes under each factor (0 = best). Multi-pass averages can crown a compromise leader.\n              </div>\n              <div v-for=\"row in perCriterionLeaders\" :key=\"row.criterion.id\" class=\"factor-leader-row\">\n                <div><div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">Factor</div><strong>{{ row.criterion.title }}</strong></div>\n                <div><div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">Top option</div><strong>{{ row.leaderTitle }}</strong></div>\n                <div>\n                  <div class=\"font-weight-bold\">{{ formatPercent(row.score) }}</div>\n                  <div class=\"text-caption\">mean rank {{ row.mean_rank != null ? Number(row.mean_rank).toFixed(2) : '—' }}</div>\n                </div>\n                <div class=\"text-caption\">\n                  {{ row.evidence }} groups\n                  <template v-if=\"row.pass_consistency != null\">\n                    <br>pass {{ asMetricPercent(row.pass_consistency) }}%\n                  </template>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n        </v-col>\n\n        <v-col cols=\"12\" lg=\"5\">\n          <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n              <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold mb-1\">Participants</div>\n              <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-4\">\n                {{ uniqueParticipants }} participants · click a row to expand individual ranking\n              </div>\n              <div v-if=\"participantRows.length\" class=\"d-flex flex-column ga-1\">\n                <div\n                  v-for=\"p in participantRows\"\n                  :key=\"p.id\"\n                  class=\"participant-row pa-2\"\n                  :class=\"{ 'is-selected': selectedParticipant?.id === p.id }\"\n                  @click=\"selectParticipant(p.id)\"\n                >\n                  <div class=\"d-flex align-center justify-space-between ga-3\">\n                    <div class=\"min-w-0\">\n                      <div class=\"font-weight-medium text-truncate\">\n                        {{ p.display_name || p.email || `Participant ${p.id}` }}\n                        <v-chip v-if=\"p.is_ai || p.source === 'ai'\" size=\"x-small\" class=\"ml-2\" color=\"primary\" variant=\"tonal\">AI</v-chip>\n                      </div>\n                      <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">{{ participantSubtitle(p) }}</div>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"d-flex flex-column align-end ga-1 flex-shrink-0\">\n                      <v-chip size=\"x-small\" variant=\"tonal\">{{ p.leaderTitle || '—' }}</v-chip>\n                      <span v-if=\"p.coherence != null\" class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">\n                        align {{ formatCoherence(p.coherence) }}\n                      </span>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                  <v-expand-transition>\n                    <div v-if=\"selectedParticipant?.id === p.id\" class=\"participant-detail-panel\" @click.stop>\n                      <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between align-center mb-3\">\n                        <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 font-weight-bold\">Individual ranking</div>\n                        <v-btn icon variant=\"text\" size=\"x-small\" @click.stop=\"selectedParticipant = null\">\n                          <i class=\"fa-solid fa-xmark\" />\n                        </v-btn>\n                      </div>\n                      <div v-if=\"selectedRanking.length\">\n                        <div v-for=\"(item, index) in selectedRanking\" :key=\"item.id\" class=\"rank-row mb-2\">\n                          <div class=\"d-flex align-center ga-3\">\n                            <div class=\"rank-number\">{{ index + 1 }}</div>\n                            <div class=\"flex-grow-1 d-flex justify-space-between\">\n                              <strong>{{ item.title }}</strong>\n                              <span>{{ formatPercent(item.score) }}</span>\n                            </div>\n                          </div>\n                        </div>\n                      </div>\n                      <div v-else class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">No ranking available for this participant.</div>\n                    </div>\n                  </v-expand-transition>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <div v-else class=\"text-medium-emphasis\">No comparisons recorded yet.</div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n\n          <v-card class=\"glass-card mt-5\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n              <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-3 align-center mb-2\">\n                <div>\n                  <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold\">Leader concentration</div>\n                  <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">\n                    How often each option is a participant's #1 (ties share #1) · {{ leaderConcentrationExplanation }}\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n                <v-select\n                  v-model=\"leaderWeighting\"\n                  :items=\"resultViewOptions\"\n                  item-title=\"title\"\n                  item-value=\"value\"\n                  label=\"View #1s by\"\n                  density=\"compact\"\n                  hide-details\n                  style=\"max-width:280px\"\n                />\n              </div>\n              <div v-for=\"row in leaderConcentrationRows\" :key=\"row.id\" class=\"mb-3\">\n                <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between text-caption mb-1\">\n                  <span>{{ row.title }}</span>\n                  <strong>\n                    {{ row.count }}\n                    <span v-if=\"leaderConcentrationParticipants\" class=\"text-medium-emphasis font-weight-regular\">\n                      / {{ leaderConcentrationParticipants }}\n                    </span>\n                  </strong>\n                </div>\n                <v-progress-linear\n                  :model-value=\"leaderConcentrationParticipants ? (row.count / leaderConcentrationParticipants) * 100 : 0\"\n                  height=\"8\"\n                  rounded\n                  color=\"secondary\"\n                />\n              </div>\n              <div v-if=\"!leaderConcentrationRows.length\" class=\"text-medium-emphasis text-caption\">\n                No participant leaders yet.\n              </div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n        </v-col>\n      </v-row>\n\n      <v-card v-if=\"showPivot\" class=\"glass-card mt-5\">\n        <v-card-text class=\"pa-5 pa-md-6\">\n          <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-3 align-start mb-4\">\n            <div>\n              <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold\">Explore by axis</div>\n              <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">\n                Choose a primary axis, optionally filter the others, sort, then select a row for supporting detail.\n                Alignment uses {{ coherenceMethodLabel }}. Filters reshape this table and the detail panel.\n              </div>\n              <div v-if=\"pivotFiltersActive\" class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2 mt-2\">\n                <v-chip\n                  v-for=\"label in activePivotFilterLabels\"\n                  :key=\"label\"\n                  size=\"x-small\"\n                  variant=\"tonal\"\n                  color=\"primary\"\n                >\n                  {{ label }}\n                </v-chip>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-3 mb-4\">\n            <v-btn-toggle v-model=\"pivotAxis\" mandatory color=\"primary\" density=\"comfortable\" divided>\n              <v-btn value=\"alternatives\">Options</v-btn>\n              <v-btn value=\"factors\" :disabled=\"factorRankings.length <= 1\">Factors</v-btn>\n              <v-btn value=\"participants\">Participants</v-btn>\n            </v-btn-toggle>\n            <v-select\n              v-if=\"pivotAxis !== 'alternatives'\"\n              v-model=\"filterAlternativeId\"\n              :items=\"altFilterItems\"\n              item-title=\"title\"\n              item-value=\"value\"\n              label=\"Filter option\"\n              density=\"compact\"\n              hide-details\n              clearable\n              style=\"min-width:180px;max-width:220px\"\n            />\n            <v-select\n              v-if=\"pivotAxis !== 'factors' && factorRankings.length > 1\"\n              v-model=\"filterFactorId\"\n              :items=\"factorFilterItems\"\n              item-title=\"title\"\n              item-value=\"value\"\n              label=\"Filter factor\"\n              density=\"compact\"\n              hide-details\n              clearable\n              style=\"min-width:180px;max-width:220px\"\n            />\n            <v-select\n              v-if=\"pivotAxis !== 'participants'\"\n              v-model=\"filterParticipantId\"\n              :items=\"participantFilterItems\"\n              item-title=\"title\"\n              item-value=\"value\"\n              label=\"Filter participant\"\n              density=\"compact\"\n              hide-details\n              clearable\n              style=\"min-width:180px;max-width:240px\"\n            />\n            <v-select\n              v-model=\"pivotSortKey\"\n              :items=\"pivotSortOptions\"\n              item-title=\"title\"\n              item-value=\"value\"\n              label=\"Sort by\"\n              density=\"compact\"\n              hide-details\n              style=\"min-width:150px;max-width:180px\"\n            />\n          </div>\n\n          <div class=\"pivot-table-wrap\">\n            <table class=\"pivot-table\">\n              <thead>\n                <tr>\n                  <th v-for=\"col in pivotColumns\" :key=\"col.key\" @click=\"setPivotSort(col.key)\">\n                    {{ col.title }}\n                    <span v-if=\"pivotSortKey === col.key\" class=\"sort-mark\">▾</span>\n                  </th>\n                </tr>\n              </thead>\n              <tbody>\n                <tr\n                  v-for=\"row in sortedPivotRows\"\n                  :key=\"row.id\"\n                  :class=\"{ selected: selectedPivotRowId === row.id }\"\n                  @click=\"selectPivotRow(row)\"\n                >\n                  <td v-for=\"col in pivotColumns\" :key=\"col.key\">\n                    {{ formatPivotCell(row, col.key) }}\n                  </td>\n                </tr>\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n            <div v-if=\"!sortedPivotRows.length\" class=\"text-medium-emphasis pa-4\">No rows for this view yet.</div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div v-if=\"pivotDetailLoading\" class=\"d-flex align-center ga-3 mt-5\">\n            <div class=\"question-group-timer\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n            <span class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis\">Loading supporting detail…</span>\n          </div>\n\n          <div v-else-if=\"pivotDetail\" class=\"pivot-detail mt-5\">\n            <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between align-center mb-3\">\n              <div>\n                <div class=\"text-subtitle-1 font-weight-bold\">{{ pivotDetail.selected_title || 'Selection' }}</div>\n                <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">\n                  {{ pivotDetail.data_points || 0 }} data points in slice\n                  · confidence {{ pivotDetail.metrics?.confidence ?? '—' }}%\n                  · stability {{ pivotDetail.metrics?.stability ?? '—' }}%\n                </div>\n                <div v-if=\"pivotDetailFilterLabels.length\" class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2 mt-2\">\n                  <v-chip\n                    v-for=\"label in pivotDetailFilterLabels\"\n                    :key=\"label\"\n                    size=\"x-small\"\n                    variant=\"tonal\"\n                    color=\"primary\"\n                  >\n                    {{ label }}\n                  </v-chip>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <v-btn icon variant=\"text\" size=\"small\" @click=\"clearPivotDetail\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-xmark\" /></v-btn>\n            </div>\n\n            <v-row dense>\n              <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n                <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-2\">Option ranking in slice</div>\n                <div\n                  v-for=\"(item, index) in (pivotDetail.option_ranking || []).slice(0, 8)\"\n                  :key=\"`opt-${item.id}`\"\n                  class=\"rank-row mb-2\"\n                >\n                  <div class=\"d-flex align-center ga-3\">\n                    <div class=\"rank-number\">{{ index + 1 }}</div>\n                    <div class=\"flex-grow-1 d-flex justify-space-between\">\n                      <strong>{{ item.title }}</strong>\n                      <span>{{ formatPercent(item.score || 0) }}</span>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </v-col>\n              <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n                <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-2\">\n                  Participants ({{ (pivotDetail.participants || []).length }})\n                </div>\n                <div\n                  v-for=\"p in (pivotDetail.participants || []).slice(0, 12)\"\n                  :key=\"`pd-${p.id}`\"\n                  class=\"rank-row mb-2\"\n                >\n                  <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between ga-2\">\n                    <strong>{{ p.display_name || p.email || `Participant ${p.id}` }}</strong>\n                    <span class=\"text-caption\">\n                      conf {{ p.confidence ?? '—' }}%\n                      <template v-if=\"p.coherence != null\"> · {{ formatCoherence(p.coherence) }}</template>\n                    </span>\n                  </div>\n                  <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">\n                    prefers {{ p.leader?.title || '—' }}\n                    <template v-if=\"p.selected_alternative_rank != null\">\n                      · selected option rank #{{ p.selected_alternative_rank }}\n                    </template>\n                    · {{ p.data_points || 0 }} pts\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              </v-col>\n            </v-row>\n          </div>\n        </v-card-text>\n      </v-card>\n    </template>\n  </div>\n</template>\n\n<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { VoteParticipant } from '~/types/api'\nimport {\n  formatPercent,\n  formatNullablePercent,\n  formatRankCi,\n  formatWeightCi,\n  formatExpectedRank,\n  scoreBarPercent,\n  scoreLeadPoints,\n  asMetricPercent,\n  effectiveCriteria,\n  mapServerRanking,\n  normalizeParticipantInfluenceMode,\n  normalizeRankingMode,\n  headlineOptionLabel,\n  optionChanceCaption,\n  polarizingCaption,\n  insightMetricChips,\n  lookupRankMap,\n  rankingFromMeanRankMap,\n  participantLeaderUnderFactor,\n  participantLeadersUnderFactor,\n  tiedLeadersFromRanking,\n} from '~/utils/ranking'\n\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst snackbar = useSnackbar()\nconst votes = useVotesApi()\nconst { isAdmin, aiFeaturesEnabled, requireAdmin } = useAuth()\nconst runningAi = ref(false)\nconst ws = useProjectWorkspace()\n\nasync function runAiBaseline() {\n  if (!requireAdmin() || !project.value) return\n  runningAi.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votes.runAiBaseline(project.value.id)\n    if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n    if (res.already_complete) snackbar.success('AI agents have already finished')\n    else snackbar.success('AI agents started. Refresh results when they finish.')\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not start AI baseline')\n  }\n  finally {\n    runningAi.value = false\n  }\n}\nconst {\n  project, altItems, factorItems, participants, report,\n  loading, settings, loadProject,\n} = ws\n\nconst analysisMode = ref<'human' | 'agents' | 'combined'>('human')\nconst canRunAiAgents = computed(() =>\n  !!aiFeaturesEnabled.value\n  && isAdmin.value\n  && !!project.value?.include_ai_agents\n  && (project.value?.ai_voter_models || []).length > 0,\n)\nconst hasAgentAnalysis = computed(() => {\n  const analysis = report.value?.analysis as Record<string, any> | undefined\n  return !!(analysis?.agents || report.value?.ai_baseline?.available)\n})\nconst viewReport = computed(() => {\n  const base = report.value || {}\n  const analysis = (base as any).analysis as Record<string, any> | undefined\n  if (!analysis) return base\n  if (analysisMode.value === 'agents' && analysis.agents) return { ...base, ...analysis.agents }\n  if (analysisMode.value === 'combined' && analysis.combined) return { ...base, ...analysis.combined }\n  if (analysis.human) return { ...base, ...analysis.human }\n  return base\n})\nconst optionAgentComparison = computed(() => {\n  const analysis = report.value?.analysis as Record<string, any> | undefined\n  return  weightSd: null,\n+  density: 'compact',\n+})\n+\n+const TOOLTIP_TEXT = 'visually shows the likely importance range of this factor'\n+\n+const model = computed<WeightRangeBarModel | null>(() =>\n+ {}\n+  return {\n+    left: `${model.value.bandStart}%`,\n+    width: `${Math.max(0, model.value.bandEnd - model.value.bandStart)}%`,\n+    background: weightRangeGradient(model.value.stops),\n+  }\n+})\n+\n+const showTickLabels = computed(() =>\n+  props.density === 'comfortable' && (model.value?.ticks.some(t => t.label != null) ?? false),\n+)\n+\n+const srText = computed(() => {\n+  const b/client/utils/weightRangeBar.test.ts\n@@ -0,0 +1,74 @@\n+import { test } from 'node:test'\n+import assert from 'node:assert/strict'\n+import {\n+  buildWeightRangeBar,\n+  sdFromWeightCi,\n+  weightRangeGradient,\n+} from './weightRangeBar.ts'\n+\n+test('sdFromWeightCi divides a 95% interval and rejects degenerate CIs', () => {\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs((sdFromWeightCi([0.1, 0.492]) ?? 0) - 0.1) < 0.001)\n+  assert.equal(sdFromWeightCi([0.2, 0.2]), null)\n+  assert.equal(sdFromWeightCi(null), null)\n+  assert.equal(sdFromWeightCi([0.1]), null)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar needs weight and derivable SD', () => {\n+  assert.equal(buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.3, weightCi95: null }), null)\n+  assert.equal(buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: null, weightCi95: [0.1, 0.3] }), null)\n+  assert.equal(buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: null, weightCi95: null }), null)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar places 7 stops fading to zero across the ±2 SD band', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.4, weightSd: 0.1 })\n+  assert.ok(m)\n+  // ±2·0.1 = 0.2 around 0.4 → band [0.2, 0.6] → start 20%, end 60%\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.bandStart - 20) < 0.01)\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.bandEnd - 60) < 0.01)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops.length, 7)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[0].offset, 0)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[0].alpha, 0)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[6].offset, 100)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[6].alpha, 0)\n+  const peak = m.stops.find(s => s.offset === 50)\n+  assert.ok(peak && peak.alpha > 0.8)\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.peakOffset - 40) < 0.01)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar clips the band to 0..1', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.95, weightSd: 0.2 })\n+  assert.ok(m)\n+  // 0.95 ±0.4 => [0.55,1] clipped\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.bandStart - 55) < 0.01)\n+  assert.equal(m.bandEnd, 100)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[m.stops.length - 1].alpha, 0)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar keeps minimal visible band for near-certain weight', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.5, weightSd: 0.001 })\n+  assert.ok(m)\n+  const spanPct = m.bandEnd - m.bandStart\n+  assert.ok(spanPct >= 1.5, `band ${spanPct} should be at least ~2%`)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar ticks are 0%..100% at 20% steps', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.4, weightSd: 0.05 })\n+  assert.ok(m)\n+  assert.deepEqual(m.ticks.map(t => t.label), ['0%', '20%', '40%', '60%', '80%', '100%'])\n+  assert.deepEqual(m.ticks.map(t => t.offset), [0, 20, 40, 60, 80, 100])\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar falls back to CI-derived SD', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.41, weightCi95: [0.14, 0.73] })\n+  assert.ok(m, 'CI fallback should render')\n+})\n+\n+test('weightRangeGradient emits rgba stops on deep-purple', () => {\n+  const css = weightRangeGradient([\n+    { offset: 0, alpha: 0 },\n+    { offset: 50, alpha: 0.88 },\n+    { offset: 100, alpha: 0 },\n+  ])\n+  assert.match(css, /^linear-gradient\\(90deg, /)\n+  assert.match(css, /rgba\\(94, 53, 177, 0\\.88\\) 50%/)\n+})\ndiff --git a/client/utils/weightRangeBar.ts b/client/utils/weightRangeBar.ts\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..8b97ea4\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/client/utils/weightRangeBar.ts\n@@ -0,0 +1,131 @@\n+/**\n+ * Geometry for the factor importance weight range bar.\n+ *\n+ * Paints a compact horizontal weight axis (0%…100%) with a color gradient peaking at\n+ * the posterior mean weight and fading to the background at ±2 SD. Pure math\n+ * only — WeightRangeBar.vue turns the model into markup.\n+ */\n+\n+export interface WeightRangeBarInput {\n+  weight?: number | null\n+  weightSd?: number | null\n+  /** 95% weight interval (0–1); used to approximate SD when weight_sd is missing. */\n+  weightCi95?: number[] | null\n+}\n+\n+export interface WeightGradientStop {\n+  /** Percent within the painted band (0–100). */\n+  offset: number\n+  /** Peak color intensity 0–1 at this stop. */\n+  alpha: number\n+}\n+\n+export interface WeightTick {\n+  /** Percent across the full axis (0–100). */\n+  offset: number\n+  /** Numeric label; null for unlabeled minor ticks. */\n+  label: string | null\n+}\n+\n+export interface WeightRangeBarModel {\n+  bandStart: number\n+  bandEnd: number\n+  stops: WeightGradientStop[]\n+  ticks: WeightTick[]\n+  /** Axis percent of the weight peak (0–100). */\n+  peakOffset: number\n+}\n+\n+/** Gradient reaches zero intensity at mean ± this many SDs. */\n+export const WEIGHT_BAR_SD_SPAN = 2\n+/** 7 gradient stops: mean, ±⅔ SD, ±4⁄3 SD, ±2 SD. */\n+export const WEIGHT_BAR_STOP_ALPHAS = [0, 0.15, 0.45, 0.88, 0.45, 0.15, 0]\n","metadata":{"output":"commit 54593f03df7e4f4ebf390ef3793d6d98efd2fd27\nAuthor: DH <dan.hoogterp@gmail.com>\nDate:   Fri Aug 21 22:20:55 2026 +0000\n\n    project and share results view pages  weightSd: null,\n+  density: 'compact',\n+})\n+\n+const TOOLTIP_TEXT = 'visually shows the likely importance range of this factor'\n+\n+const model = computed<WeightRangeBarModel | null>(() =>\n+ }\n+})\n+\n+const showTickLabels = computed(() =>\n+  props.density === 'comfortable' && (model.value?.ticks.some(t => t.label != null) ?? false),\n+)\n+\n+const srText = computed(() => {\n+  const b/client/utils/weightRangeBar.test.ts\n@@ -0,0 +1,74 @@\n+import { test } from 'node:test'\n+import assert from 'node:assert/strict'\n+import {\n+  buildWeightRangeBar,\n+  sdFromWeightCi,\n+  weightRangeGradient,\n+} from './weightRangeBar.ts'\n+\n+test('sdFromWeightCi divides a 95% interval and rejects degenerate CIs', () => {\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs((sdFromWeightCi([0.1, 0.492]) ?? 0) - 0.1) < 0.001)\n+  assert.equal(sdFromWeightCi([0.2, 0.2]), null)\n+  assert.equal(sdFromWeightCi(null), null)\n+  assert.equal(sdFromWeightCi([0.1]), null)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar needs weight and derivable SD', () => {\n+  assert.equal(buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.3, weightCi95: null }), null)\n+  assert.equal(buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: null, weightCi95: [0.1, 0.3] }), null)\n+  assert.equal(buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: null, weightCi95: null }), null)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar places 7 stops fading to zero across the ±2 SD band', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.4, weightSd: 0.1 })\n+  assert.ok(m)\n+  // ±2·0.1 = 0.2 around 0.4 → band [0.2, 0.6] → start 20%, end 60%\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.bandStart - 20) < 0.01)\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.bandEnd - 60) < 0.01)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops.length, 7)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[0].offset, 0)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[0].alpha, 0)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[6].offset, 100)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[6].alpha, 0)\n+  const peak = m.stops.find(s => s.offset === 50)\n+  assert.ok(peak && peak.alpha > 0.8)\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.peakOffset - 40) < 0.01)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar clips the band to 0..1', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.95, weightSd: 0.2 })\n+  assert.ok(m)\n+  // 0.95 ±0.4 => [0.55,1] clipped\n+  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.bandStart - 55) < 0.01)\n+  assert.equal(m.bandEnd, 100)\n+  assert.equal(m.stops[m.stops.length - 1].alpha, 0)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar keeps minimal visible band for near-certain weight', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.5, weightSd: 0.001 })\n+  assert.ok(m)\n+  const spanPct = m.bandEnd - m.bandStart\n+  assert.ok(spanPct >= 1.5, `band ${spanPct} should be at least ~2%`)\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar ticks are 0%..100% at 20% steps', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.4, weightSd: 0.05 })\n+  assert.ok(m)\n+  assert.deepEqual(m.ticks.map(t => t.label), ['0%', '20%', '40%', '60%', '80%', '100%'])\n+  assert.deepEqual(m.ticks.map(t => t.offset), [0, 20, 40, 60, 80, 100])\n+})\n+\n+test('buildWeightRangeBar falls back to CI-derived SD', () => {\n+  const m = buildWeightRangeBar({ weight: 0.41, weightCi95: [0.14, 0.73] })\n+  assert.ok(m, 'CI fallback should render')\n+})\n+\n+test('weightRangeGradient emits rgba stops on deep-purple', () => {\n+  const css = weightRangeGradient([\n+    { offset: 0, alpha: 0 },\n+    { offset: 50, alpha: 0.88 },\n+    { offset: 100, alpha: 0 },\n+  ])\n+  assert.match(css, /^linear-gradient\\(90deg, /)\n+  assert.match(css, /rgba\\(94, 53, 177, 0\\.88\\) 50%/)\n+})\ndiff --git a/client/utils/weightRangeBar.ts b/client/utils/weightRangeBar.ts\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..8b97ea4\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/client/utils/weightRangeBar.ts\n@@ -0,0 +1,131 @@\n+/**\n+ * Geometry for the factor importance weight range bar.\n+ *\n+ * Paints a compact horizontal weight axis (0%…100%) with a color gradient peaking at\n+ * the posterior mean weight and fading to the background at ±2 SD. Pure math\n+ * only — WeightRangeBar.vue turns the model into markup.\n+ */\n+\n+export interface WeightRangeBarInput {\n+  weight?: number | null\n+  weightSd?: number | null\n+  /** 95% weight interval (0–1); used to approximate SD when weight_sd is missing. */\n+  weightCi95?: number[] | null\n+}\n+\n+export interface WeightGradientStop {\n+  /** Percent within the painted band (0–100). */\n+  offset: number\n+  /** Peak color intensity 0–1 at this stop. */\n+  alpha: number\n+}\n+\n+export interface WeightTick {\n+  /** Percent across the full axis (0–100). */\n+  offset: number\n+  /** Numeric label; null for unlabeled minor ticks. */\n+  label: string | null\n+}\n+\n+export interface WeightRangeBarModel {\n+  bandStart: number\n+  bandEnd: number\n+  stops: WeightGradientStop[]\n+  ticks: WeightTick[]\n+  /** Axis percent of the weight peak (0–100). */\n+  peakOffset: number\n+}\n+\n+/** Gradient reaches zero intensity at mean ± this many SDs. */\n+export const WEIGHT_BAR_SD_SPAN = 2\n+/** 7 gradient stops: mean, ±⅔ SD, ±4⁄3 SD, ±2 SD. */\n+export const WEIGHT_BAR_STOP_ALPHAS = [0, 0.15, 0.45, 0.88, 0.45, 0.15, 0]\n","exit":0,"truncated":false},"title":"git show 54593f0 | head -500","time":{"start":1787395845801,"end":1787395845812}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a029182ad57a029de21304c5391bd8"}},"id":"prt_029182b13001AE261V7VlKF30U","sessionID":"ses_fd6e8f55dffeLGvRwdq47PegZZ","messageID":"msg_029182335001ceGyOUBmLjWWTd"}}

Then adjust the prompts so they are shown in successive lines with clear 1. and 2. prefixes.\",\n1044:           \"expandedDescription\": \"Migrate the default AI agent voting prompt from a single `choice_line` placeholder to distinct `choice_1` and `choice_2` placeholders to enable more precise, per-choice presentation control. Update the default prompt template(s) and rendering logic to display the two choices on successive lines with explicit `1.` and `2.` prefixes for unambiguous selection. Scope is limited to prompting/presentation; no changes to toteboard/board storage, persistence, or voting tally logic.\\n\\n- Scope: Locate default AI voting prompt template(s)/config using `choice_line` and replace with `choice_1`/`choice_2`; update prompt assembly to emit `1. {choice_1}` and `2. {choice_2}` on consecutive lines.\\n- Scope: Update any docs, examples, or tests referencing `choice_line` and verify rendered prompt output; no file/queue/toteboard changes.\\n- Key consideration: Backward compatibility for existing/overridden prompts still referencing `choice_line` — deprecate vs. fallback alias/dual-support during transition.\\n- Key consideration: Consistent formatting, escaping/truncation, and injection safety for choice content across LLM providers; preserve line breaks/prefixes.\\n- Open question: Whether prefix style (`1.`/`2.` vs `1:`) and line separation should be hard-coded or made configurable, and if >2-choice variants need future-proofing.\",\n1045:           \"type\": \"task\",\n1046:           \"facets\": [],\n1047:           \"notes\": \"\",\n1048:           \"codeReviewGuide\": \"\",\n1049: Keep `choice_line` as deprecated fallback that auto-splits into `choice_1`/`choice_2`? **Recommended: Yes, temporary fallback with `utils_show` deprecation warning to avoid breaking custom prompts/env overrides.**\\n2.  Confirm exact format is `1. ` and `2. ` on two successive lines with no extra blank line? **Recommended: Yes, `1. {choice_1}\\\\n2. {choice_2}` as default (single `\\\\n`, no blank line), hard-coded.**\\n3.  Apply only to default prompt or also migrate any user-customizable voting prompt templates? **Recommended: Default only (system-level templates in `config_settings.py`), but add migration note in `AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD` for any env-overridden custom templates.**\"\n1228:         },\n1229:         {\n1230:           \"id\": \"project-factor-settings-lkb6vk\",\n1231:           \"kind\": \"item\",\n1232:           \"title\": \"Project Factor Settings\",\n1233:           \"briefDescription\": \"In addition to a generate button for each factor question, add a 'generate missing factor questions' button near the top that will iteratively generate each empty factor question field.\",\n1234:           \"expandedDescription\": \"Add a bulk-generation control to the Project Factor Settings view that complements the existing per-factor \\\"Generate\\\" button: a \\\"Generate Missing Factor Questions\\\" button placed prominently near the top of the factor list/form header that, when clicked, scans all factor question fields and sequentially invokes the existing generation flow for each empty field until all missing questions are populated.\\n\\n*   **Scope:** Add top-level button to Project Factor Settings UI near header/toolbar; visible only when at least one factor question is empty; iterates over empty fields in order and calls existing per-factor generate API/logic reusing current prompts and project context.\\n*   **Behavior & UX:** Sequential (not parallel) generation with per-field loading state, overall progress indication, and disabling of both bulk and individual generate buttons during run; already-filled fields are left untouched; cancellation/interruption leaves completed generations persisted.\\n*   **State & Error Handling:** Handle partial failures gracefully (skip failed factor, continue to next, surface inline error/toast with retry affordance); respect rate limits and prevent double-click; optimistic update only on success.\\n*   **Key Considerations:** Reuse existing generation endpoint, auth, and prompt context to ensure consistency; ensure idempotency and avoid overwriting user-edited questions; add telemetry/logging for bulk vs single generation.\\n*   **Out of Scope:** No changes to factor definition/schema, prompt engineering, or bulk regeneration/overwrite of non-empty questions.\",\n1235:           \"type\": Then adjust the prompts so they are shown in successive lines with clear 1. and 2. prefixes.\",\n          \"expandedDescription\": \"Migrate the default AI agent voting prompt from a single `choice_line` placeholder to distinct `choice_1` and `choice_2` placeholders to enable more precise, per-choice presentation control. Update the default prompt template(s) and rendering logic to display the two choices on successive lines with explicit `1.` and `2.` prefixes for unambiguous selection. Scope is limited to prompting/presentation; no changes to toteboard/board storage, persistence, or voting tally logic.\\n\\n- Scope: Locate default AI voting prompt template(s)/config using `choice_line` and replace with `choice_1`/`choice_2`; update prompt assembly to emit `1. {choice_1}` and `2. {choice_2}` on consecutive lines.\\n- Scope: Update any docs, examples, or tests referencing `choice_line` and verify rendered prompt output; no file/queue/toteboard changes.\\n- Key consideration: Backward compatibility for existing/overridden prompts still referencing `choice_line` — deprecate vs. fallback alias/dual-support during transition.\\n- Key consideration: Consistent formatting, escaping/truncation, and injection safety for choice content across LLM providers; preserve line breaks/prefixes.\\n- Open question: Whether prefix style (`1.`/`2.` vs `1:`) and line separation should be hard-coded or made configurable, and if >2-choice variants need \"title\": \"Project Factor Settings\",\n          \"briefDescription\": \"In addition to a generate button for each factor question, add a 'generate missing factor questions' button near the top that will iteratively generate each empty factor question field.\",\n          \"expandedDescription\": \"Add a bulk-generation control to the Project Factor Settings view that complements the existing per-factor \\\"Generate\\\" button: a \\\"Generate Missing Factor Questions\\\" button placed prominently near the top of the factor list/form header that, when clicked, scans all factor question fields and sequentially invokes the existing generation flow for each empty field until all missing questions are populated.\\n\\n*   **Scope:** Add top-level button to Project Factor Settings UI near header/toolbar; visible only when at least one factor question is empty; iterates over empty fields in order and calls existing per-factor generate API/logic reusing current prompts and project context.\\n*   **Behavior & UX:** Sequential (not parallel) generation with per-field loading state, overall progress indication, and disabling of both bulk and individual generate buttons during run; already-filled fields are left untouched; cancellation/interruption leaves completed generations persisted.\\n*   **State & Error Handling:** Handle partial failures gracefully (skip failed factor, continue to next, surface inline error/toast with retry affordance); respect rate limits and prevent double-click; optimistic update only on success.\\n*   **Key Considerations:** Reuse existing generation endpoint, auth, and prompt context to ensure consistency; ensure idempotency and avoid overwriting user-edited questions; add telemetry/logging for bulk vs single generation.\\n*   **Out of Scope:** No changes to factor definition/schema, prompt engineering, or bulk regeneration/overwrite of non-empty questions.\",\n          \"type\": \"task\",\n          \"facets\": [],\n isAdmin, aiFeaturesEnabled } = useAuth()\n223: const runningAi = ref(false)\n224: \n225: async function runAiBaseline() {\n226:   if (!requireAdmin() || !project.value) return\n227:   if (activeAlts.value.length < 2) {\n228:     snackbar.error('Save at least two options before running AI agents')\n229:     return\n230:   }\n231:   runningAi.value = true\n232:   try {\n233:     const res = await votesApi.runAiBaseline(project.value.id)\n234:     if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n235:     if (res.already_complete) snackbar.success('AI agents have already finished')\n236:     else snackbar.success('AI agents started. Results appear when they finish.')\n237:     await loadSummary(true)\n238:   }\n239:   catch (e: unknown) {\n240:     snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not start AI agents')\n241:   }\n242:   finally {\n243:     runningAi.value = false\n244:   }\n245: }\n246: \n247: const loading = ref(true)\n248: const project = ref<CustomerProject | null>(null)\n249: const alternatives = = ref(0)\n254: const maxComparisonsEst = ref(0)\n255: const activeParticipantCount = ref(0)\n256: const aiAgentCounts = ref<AiAgentCounts>({ requested: 0, in_flight: 0, completed: 0 })\n257: const aiParticipants = ref<AiParticipantStatusItem[]>([])\n261: const myMetrics = ref<ProjectListMetrics>({})\n262: const pollTimer = ref<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null)\n263: let pollBusy = false\n264: \n265: const activeAlts = computed(() => alternatives.value.filter(a => !a.disabled))\n266: const activeFactors = computed(() => factors.value.filter(f => !f.disabled))\n267: const remainingTimeCopy = computed(() => projectRemainingTimeCopy(project.value?.end_time))\n268: const inputClosed = computed(() => !!project.value?.disabled || collectionIsClosed(project.value?.end_time))\n269: const canRunAiAgents = computed(() =>\n270:   !!aiFeaturesEnabled.value\n271:   && isAdmin.value\n272:   && !!project.value?.include_ai_agents\n273:   && (project.value?.ai_voter_models || []).length > 0,\n274: )\n275: const showHumanParticipants = computed(() =>\n276:   humanActive.value.length > 0 || humanPending.value.length > 0 || humanAnonymous.value.length > 0,\n277: )\n278: \n279: function responseCopy(n: number) {\n280:   return n === 1 ? '1 response' : `${n} responses`\n281: }\n282: function daysCopy(n: number) {\n283:   return n === 1 ? '1 day' : `${n} days`\n284: }\n285: function activatedCopy(n: number) {\n286:   return n === 1 ? '1 activated' : `${n} activated`\n287: }\n288: function pairCopy(n: number) {\n289:   return n === 1 ? '1 pair answered' : `${n} pairs answered`\n290: }\n291: function aiStatusLabel(status: string) {\n292:   if (status === 'in_progress') return 'in progress'\n293:   return status || 'queued'\n294: }\n295: function aiStatusColor(status: string) {\n296:   if (status === 'complete') return 'success'\n297:   if (status === 'failed') return 'error'\n298:   if (status === 'in_progress') return 'primary'\n299:   return undefined\n300: }\n301: \n302: function applySummary(res: ProjectPageSummaryResult) {\n303:   project.value = res.project || null\n304:   alternatives.value = res.alternatives || []\n305:   factors.value = res.factors || []\n306:   myObservationCount.value = res.my_observation_count || 0\n307:   participantCount.value = res.participant_count || 0\n308:   targetComparisonsEst.value = res.target_comparisons_est || 0\n309:   maxComparisonsEst.value = res.max_comparisons_est || 0\n310:   activeParticipantCount.value = res.active_participant_count || 0\n311:   aiAgentCounts.value = res.ai_agent_counts || { requested: 0, in_flight: 0, completed: 0 }\n312:   humanActive.value = res.human_participants?.active || []\n313:   humanPending.value = res.human_participants?.pending || []\n314:   humanAnonymous.value = res.human_participants?.anonymous_shares || []\n315:   aiParticipants.value = res.ai_participants || []\n316:   myMetrics.value = res.my_metrics || {}\n317: }\n318: \n319: function syncAiPoll() {\n320:   const need = (aiAgentCounts.value?.in_flight || 0) > 0\n321:   if (need && !pollTimer.value) {\n322:     pollTimer.value = setInterval(() => { void loadSummary(true) }, 4000)\n323:   }\n324:   else if (!need && pollTimer.value) {\n325:     clearInterval(pollTimer.value)\n326:     pollTimer.value = null\n327:   }\n328: }\n329: \n330: async function loadSummary(silent = false) {\n331:   if (pollBusy) return\n332:   pollBusy = true\n333:   if (!silent) loading.value = true\n334:   try {\n335:     const res = await api.getProjectPageSummary(Number(route.params.id))\n336:     if (res.failure_reason || !res.project) throw new Error(res.failure_reason || 'Project not found')\n337:     applySummary(res)\n338:   }\n339:   catch (e: unknown) {\n340:     if (!silent) snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to load project')\n341:   }\n342:   finally {\n343:     pollBusy = false\n344:     if (!silent) loading.value = false\n345:     syncAiPoll()\n346:   }\n347: }\n348: \n349: const nextAction = computed(() => {\n350:   const id = project.value?.id\n351:   if (!id) return { title: '', detail: '', label: '', to: '/projects' }\n352:   if (activeAlts.value.length < 2) {\n353:     return {\n354:       title: 'Complete project setup',\n355:       detail: 'Add at least two realistic options before collecting input.',\n356:       label: 'Continue setup',\n357:       to: `/projects/${id}/edit`,\n358:     }\n359:   }\n360:   if (inputClosed.value) {\n361:     return {\n362:       title: 'Review current results',\n363:       detail: 'Comparison collection is closed. Results remain available.',\n364:       label: 'View results',\n365:       to: `/projects/${id}/results`,\n366:     }\n367:   }\n368:   if (myObservationCount.value === 0 && participantCount.value === 0) {\n369:     return {\n370:       title: 'Begin collecting input',\n371:       detail: 'Invite people or start comparing yourself.',\n372:       label: 'Start comparing',\n373:       to: `/projects/${id}/probe`,\n374:     }\n375:   }\n376:   return {\n377:     title: 'Review current results',\n378:     detail: `Confidence is ${metricLabel(Number(myMetrics.value.confidence ?? 0))}. <v-card v-if=\"aiAgentCounts.requested > 0\" class=\"glass-card mb-5\">\n        <v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n          <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold mb-3\" style=\"font-family:Manrope,Inter,sans-serif\">AI participants</div>\n          <div v-for=\"row in aiParticipants\" :key=\"row.model_key\" class=\"rank-row mb-2\">\n            <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between align-center ga-3\">\n              <div class=\"min-w-0\">\n                <strong>{{ row.display_name }}</strong>\n                <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">\n                  {{ pairCopy(row.pairs_answered) }}\n                  <template v-if=\"row.pairs_remaining > 0\"> · {{ row.pairs_remaining }} remaining</template>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <v-chip size=\"small\" variant=\"tonal\" :color=\"aiStatusColor(row.status)\">{{ aiStatusLabel(row.status) }}</v-chip>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </v-card-text>\n      </v-card>\n\n      <v-row>\n        <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n          <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n              <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between align-center mb-3\">\n                <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold\" style=\"font-family:Manrope,Inter,sans-serif\">Options</div>\n                <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" color=\"primary\" :to=\"`/projects/${project.id}/edit`\">Edit</v-btn>\n              </div>\n              <div v-if=\"!activeAlts.length\" class=\"text-medium-emphasis\">\n                Add at least two options before starting comparisons.\n              </div>\n              <div v-for=\"a in activeAlts\" :key=\"a.id\" class=\"rank-row mb-2\">\n                <strong>{{ a.title }}</strong>\n                <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">{{ a.description }}</div>\n              </div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n        </v-col>\n        <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n          <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n            <v-card-text class=\"pa-5\">\n              <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between align-center mb-3\">\n                <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold\" style=\"font-family:Manrope,Inter,sans-serif\">Factors</div>\n                <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" color=\"primary\" :to=\"`/projects/${project.id}/edit`\">Edit</v-btn>\n              </div>\n              <div v-if=\"!activeFactors.length\" class=\"template-note\">\n                <i class=\"fa-solid fa-circle-check mt-1\" />\n                <div>\n                  <b>Overall only</b><br>\n                  Participants will compare options as a whole without separate factor prompts.\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <div v-for=\"f in activeFactors\" :key=\"f.id\" class=\"rank-row mb-2\">\n                <strong>{{ f.title }}</strong>\n                <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">{{ f.description }}</div>\n              </div>\n            </v-card-text>\n          </v-card>\n        </v-col>\n      </v-row>\n    </template>\n  </div>\n</template>\n\n<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type {\n  AiAgentCounts,\n  AiParticipantStatusItem,\n  AnonymousShareItem,\n  CustomerProject,\n  HumanParticipantActiveItem,\n  HumanParticipantPendingItem,\n  ProjectPageContentItem,\n  ProjectListMetrics,\n  ProjectPageSummaryResult,\n} from '~/types/api'\nimport { metricLabel } from '~/utils/ranking'\nimport { collectionIsClosed, projectRemainingTimeCopy } from '~/utils/projectEndTime'\n\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst snackbar = useSnackbar()\nconst api = useProjectsApi()\nconst votesApi = useVotesApi()\nconst { requireAdmin, isAdmin, aiFeaturesEnabled } = useAuth()\nconst runningAi = ref(false)\n\nasync function runAiBaseline() {\n  if (!requireAdmin() || !project.value) return\n  if (activeAlts.value.length < 2) {\n    snackbar.error('Save at least two options before running AI agents')\n    return\n  }\n  runningAi.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await votesApi.runAiBaseline(project.value.id)\n    if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n    if (res.already_complete) snackbar.success('AI agents have already finished')\n    else snackbar.success('AI agents started. Results appear when they finish.')\n    await loadSummary(true)\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not start AI agents')\n  }\n  finally {\n    runningAi.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nconst loading = ref(true)\nconst project = ref<CustomerProject | null>(null)\nconst alternatives = ref<ProjectPageContentItem[]>([])\nconst factors = ref<ProjectPageContentItem[]>([])\nconst myObservationCount = ref(0)\nconst participantCount = ref(0)\nconst targetComparisonsEst = ref(0)\nconst maxComparisonsEst = ref(0)\nconst activeParticipantCount = ref(0)\nconst aiAgentCounts = ref<AiAgentCounts>({ requested: 0, in_flight: 0, completed: 0 })\nconst humanActive = ref<HumanParticipantActiveItem[]>([])\nconst humanPending = ref<HumanParticipantPendingItem[]>([])\nconst humanAnonymous = ref<AnonymousShareItem[]>([])\nconst aiParticipants = ref<AiParticipantStatusItem[]>([])\nconst myMetrics = ref<ProjectListMetrics>({})\nconst pollTimer = ref<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null)\nlet pollBusy = false\n\nconst activeAlts = computed(() => alternatives.value.filter(a => !a.disabled))\nconst activeFactors = computed(() => factors.value.filter(f => !f.disabled))\nconst remainingTimeCopy = computed(() => projectRemainingTimeCopy(project.value?.end_time))\nconst inputClosed = computed(() => !!project.value?.disabled || collectionIsClosed(project.value?.end_time))\nconst canRunAiAgents = computed(() =>\n  !!aiFeaturesEnabled.value\n  && isAdmin.value\n  && !!project.value?.include_ai_agents\n  && (project.value?.ai_voter_models || []).length > 0,\n)\nconst showHumanParticipants = computed(() =>\n  humanActive.value.length > 0 || humanPending.value.length > 0 || humanAnonymous.value.length > 0,\n)\n\nfunction responseCopy(n: number) {\n  return n === 1 ? '1 response' : `${n} responses`\n}\nfunction daysCopy(n: number) {\n  return n === 1 ? '1 day' : `${n} days`\n}\nfunction activatedCopy(n: number) {\n  return n === 1 ? '1 activated' : `${n} activated`\n}\nfunction pairCopy(n: number) {\n  return n === 1 ? '1 pair answered' : `${n} pairs answered`\n}\nfunction aiStatusLabel(status: string) {\n  if (status === 'in_progress') return 'in progress'\n  return status || 'queued'\n}\nfunction aiStatusColor(status: string) {\n  if (status === 'complete') return 'success'\n  if (status === 'failed') return 'error'\n  if (status === 'in_progress') return 'primary'\n  return undefined\n}\n\nfunction applySummary(res: ProjectPageSummaryResult) {\n  project.value = res.project || null\n  alternatives.value = res.alternatives || []\n  factors.value = res.factors || []\n  myObservationCount.value = res.my_observation_count || 0\n  participantCount.value = res.participant_count || 0\n  targetComparisonsEst.value = res.target_comparisons_est || 0\n  maxComparisonsEst.value = res.max_comparisons_est || 0\n  activeParticipantCount.value = res.active_participant_count || 0\n  aiAgentCounts.value = res.ai_agent_counts || { requested: 0, in_flight: 0, completed: 0 }\n  humanActive.value = res.human_participants?.active || []\n  humanPending.value = res.human_participants?.pending || []\n  humanAnonymous.value = res.human_participants?.anonymous_shares || []\n  aiParticipants.value = res.ai_participants || []\n  myMetrics.value = res.my_metrics || {}\n}\n\nfunction syncAiPoll() {\n  const need = (aiAgentCounts.value?.in_flight || 0) > 0\n  if (need && !pollTimer.value) {\n    pollTimer.value = setInterval(() => { void loadSummary(true) }, 4000)\n  }\n  else if (!need && pollTimer.value) {\n    clearInterval(pollTimer.value)\n    pollTimer.value = null\n  }\n}\n\nasync function loadSummary(silent = false) {\n  if (pollBusy) return\n  pollBusy = true\n  if (!silent) loading.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await api.getProjectPageSummary(Number(route.params.id))\n    if (res.failure_reason || !res.project) throw new Error(res.failure_reason || 'Project not found')\n    applySummary(res)\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    if (!silent) snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to load project')\n  }\n  finally {\n    pollBusy = false\n    if (!silent) loading.value = false\n    syncAiPoll()\n  }\n}\n\nconst nextAction = computed(() => {\n  const id = project.value?.id\n  if (!id) return { title: '', detail: '', label: '', to: '/projects' }\n  if (activeAlts.value.length < 2) {\n    return {\n      title: 'Complete project setup',\n      detail: 'Add at least two realistic options before collecting input.',\n      label: 'Continue setup',\n      to: `/projects/${id}/edit`,\n    }\n  }\n  if (inputClosed.value) {\n    return {\n      title: 'Review current results',\n      detail: 'Comparison collection is closed. Results remain available.',\n      label: 'View results',\n      to: `/projects/${id}/results`,\n    }\n  }\n  if (myObservationCount.value === 0 && participantCount.value === 0) {\n    return {\n      title: 'Begin collecting input',\n      detail: 'Invite people or start comparing yourself.',\n      label: 'Start comparing',\n      to: `/projects/${id}/probe`,\n    }\n  }\n  return {\n    title: 'Review current results',\n    detail: `Confidence is ${metricLabel(Number(myMetrics.value.confidence ?? 0))}. Open results to explain the outcome.`,\n    label: 'View results',\n    to: `/projects/${id}/results`,\n  }\n})\n\nfunction makeDuplicate() {\n  if (!requireAdmin() || !project.value) return\n  navigateTo(`/projects/0/edit?from=${project.value.id}`)\n}\n\nonMounted(() => {\n  void loadSummary()\n})\nonUnmounted(() => {\n  if (pollTimer.value) {\n    clearInterval(pollTimer.value)\n    pollTimer.value = null\n  }\n})\n</script>\n\n<style scoped>\n@media (max-width: 960px) {\n  .summary-hero {\n    grid-template-columns: 1fr !important;\n  }\n}\n</style>","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":406,"totalLines":406,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/pages/projects/[id]/index.vue","time":{"start":1787395907068,"end":1787395907142}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a0291919617b10a686eb746a54b11b"}},"id":"prt_02919199e001e2TTvYVoewC6Qn","sessionID":"ses_fd6e8f55dffeLGvRwdq47PegZZ","messageID":"msg_02918d80d001XIvHnU8y9NZtG1"}}
